# Retired promises

This page lists each behavior that `main` promised and that the native cutover no longer promises. Use it to review the retirements before the merge. The data comes from these files:

- [`tools/lune/verification_parity.json`](../../tools/lune/verification_parity.json), class b and class c contracts.
- The strengthening results of the parity work (weaker contracts A, B and C). They retire parts of covered contracts.
- The `tools/lune/coverage_*.json` retirement reasons.

The baseline is `main` at `a8c88956`. The branch is `codex/compose-ui-simplification` at `a358bed6`. Current `main` is `bc9a56a6`. It has the same public model as the baseline and adds to it.

## Summary

| Kind | Contracts | Main cases |
|---|---:|---:|
| Class b: the Roblox Engine or Compose owns the mechanism | 254 | 1295 |
| Class b with no candidate test and no live record | 47 | 199 |
| Class c: the feature or the code is deleted | 851 | 6010 |
| Restored before the merge: moved from class b or c to class a | 21 | 177 |
| Parts of covered contracts that the strengthening work retired | 117 | - |
| Owner decisions (next section) | 34 | - |

Most class c contracts tested the deleted solver, renderer, focus graph, input system, presenter and paint layer. Their retirement is correct when the public API no longer makes the promise. The next section lists the retirements that a player or a game author can see.

## Owner decisions

Each item below removed a behavior that a player or a game author uses. The owner decided each item. The "Decision" line gives the result:

- Restored: Facet has the behavior again. [Restored before the merge](#restored-before-the-merge) names the change and its tests.
- Restored by another change: a separate branch restores the behavior.
- Accepted: the retirement stays. The line names the native replacement that a game uses.

### 1. Screen-level entry focus

- Main promised: When a screen or a surface appeared, Facet put the focus on a declared or first control (`UI.focusSection` `focusOnAppear`/`returnFocus`, presenter `initialFocus`), and the initial focus showed its ring.
- Main evidence: 4 contracts, 33 main cases: `apps-153` (7, class c, `focus_section_appear`); `apps-170` (22, class c, `keyboard_navigation`); `inputs-04` (3, class b, `auto_input`); `apps-187` (1, class b, `navigation_groups`).
- Strengthening notes that retire part of a covered contract: `apps-159`, `mech2-104`.
- PR status: The PR gives entry selection only to modal controls (Alert, Sheet, Menu) and to collections that you give a focus option. On an ordinary screen, a gamepad or keyboard player starts with no selection until the engine picks one. `docs/guide/07-input.md` promises entry only for modals.
- Current main `bc9a56a6` still ships this.
- Decision: Restored. The first D-pad press selects the first control of the active screen. [Restored before the merge](#restored-before-the-merge) names the tests.

### 2. Nearest-survivor focus after a removal

- Main promised: When the focused row or control was removed, focus moved to the nearest surviving neighbour (`focusPolicy`, index policy).
- Main evidence: 2 contracts, 18 main cases: `collections-260` (17, class c, `virtual_list_focus_policy`); `collections-306` (1, class c, `virtualization`).
- Strengthening notes that retire part of a covered contract: `apps-147`, `apps-185`.
- PR status: Compose `createFocusScope` clears the focus or moves it to the first row. A gamepad player loses the position in a long list after a delete.
- Current main `bc9a56a6` still ships this.
- Decision: Restored. A removed control gives the selection to its nearest neighbour, and a collection keeps it on the next row by key. [Restored before the merge](#restored-before-the-merge) names the tests.

### 3. Tab and Shift+Tab traversal

- Main promised: Tab and Shift+Tab walked all focus stops in order, with entry, group crossing, wrap and skip rules, also inside the radial ring.
- Main evidence: 4 contracts, 22 main cases: `apps-158` (15, class b, `keyboard_navigation`); `apps2-169` (1, class b, `playlist_columns`); `apps2-222` (1, class b, `recipes_common`); `apps-151` (5, class b, `focus_chrome`).
- Strengthening notes that retire part of a covered contract: `apps2-210`.
- PR status: The PR binds no Tab key (`Button` refuses Tab as a shortcut). Roblox engine selection moves by direction only. The b-class record says `nativeEvidence: none`. The live run `artifacts/studio-live/needs_live_input-power-off-traversal-portrait-largest.json` records the check `Tab reaches After` as failed: Tab selected no control, while DPadDown did.
- Current main `bc9a56a6` still ships this.
- Decision: Restored. Tab and Shift+Tab walk the controls in layout order inside the active modal or screen. [Restored before the merge](#restored-before-the-merge) names the tests.

### 4. Ten-foot (viewing distance) profile, including the ten-foot focus ring

- Main promised: A distant-TV profile changed the type ladder, the hit floor, the overscan margins, the 66 px focus floor and the focus ring, stacked Alert actions and centred Sheet panels.
- Main evidence: 6 contracts, 83 main cases: `mech2-93` (25, class c, `paradigm_tenfoot`); `mech3-62` (48, class c, `ten_foot_metrics`); `navigation-2-21` (1, class c, `distance_gamepad`); `navigation-1-15` (1, class c, `alert`); `navigation-4-46` (1, class c, `sheet`); `apps2-96` (7, class b, `gallery_chrome`).
- Strengthening notes that retire part of a covered contract: `navigation-1-13`, `navigation-1-01`, `navigation-4-41`, `apps-190`, `paint-85`, `apps2-134`, `inputs-53`.
- PR status: The PR has no environment distance fact. The gallery keeps only a `UIScale` 1.5 preview. Console players get desktop density unless each game scales its own UI.
- Current main `bc9a56a6` still ships this.
- Decision: Accepted. The engine selection highlight and `GuiObject.SelectionImageObject` replace the ten-foot focus ring. A game that needs a distance profile scales its own UI, for example with `UIScale`.

### 5. Theme-owned focus indicator

- Main promised: A theme package set the focus ring or glow, its colour, its corners and its fit around skinned art.
- Main evidence: 6 contracts, 23 main cases: `paint-73` (5, class b, `native_style_scenario`); `themes-P2-31` (8, class c, `theme_chrome`); `themes-P5-20` (2, class b, `theme_reference_packages`); `themes-P5-43` (2, class b, `review_theme_corners`); `themes-P4-15` (3, class b, `theme_layer_application`); `apps-166` (3, class b, `keyboard_navigation`).
- Strengthening notes that retire part of a covered contract: `mech1-91`.
- PR status: The PR uses the engine default selection image (`SelectionImageObject` is not set). `theme_types.SkinLayer.kind` still lists `glow`, and two shipped packages still declare focus art that nothing reads (`themes-P2-31` removal text).
- Current main `bc9a56a6` still ships this.
- Decision: Accepted. The engine selection highlight is the focus indicator. A game that needs its own indicator sets `SelectionImageObject` on the control.

### 6. The application handle

- Main promised: `Facet.new()` made one application with `controls`, `mount`, `environment`, `presenter`, `onFrame`, `presentModal`, `presentAnchored`, `presentToast`, `installTheme` and `dispose`, and shut them down in a fixed order.
- Main evidence: 4 contracts, 39 main cases: `mech2-13` (10, class c, `host_core_lifetime`); `mech1-64` (21, class c, `client_host`); `mech2-94` (3, class c, `parentless_build_owner`); `apps2-06` (5, class c, `consumer_standalone`).
- Strengthening notes that retire part of a covered contract: `apps-125`.
- PR status: The caller now makes the runtime, the ScreenGui, the StyleSheet and the StyleLink, and calls `runtime.mount`, `stop` and `runtime:dispose`. Every main screen must change (ergonomics audit, section 5).
- Current main `bc9a56a6` still ships this.
- Decision: Accepted. The game uses the Compose runtime, `runtime.mount` (the mount helper), `RunService` for frame work and Compose animation for motion.

### 7. Adaptive environment facts

- Main promised: `app.environment` and `Facet.adaptive` published size class, orientation, interaction classes, display size, safe areas, locale, preview profiles and a theme choice by input class (`selectBy`).
- Main evidence: 9 contracts, 82 main cases: `mech1-02` (36, class c, `adaptive`); `mech3-11` (7, class c, `renderer`); `apps-236` (8, class c, `adaptive_defaults`); `apps2-233` (4, class c, `spatial`); `apps-132` (6, class c, `preview`); `apps2-04` (2, class c, `consumer_standalone`); `apps2-75` (1, class c, `examples_games`); `themes-P3-36` (13, class c, `theme_selectby`); `apps2-151` (5, class c, `perf_lab`).
- Strengthening notes that retire part of a covered contract: `mech1-121`.
- PR status: Controls read `UserInputService.PreferredInput` and native `AbsoluteSize` directly. A screen that must choose between two arrangements observes `AbsoluteSize` itself (`docs/guide/15-adaptive-recipes.md`). AGENTS.md on main still tells authors to read `app.environment`.
- Current main `bc9a56a6` still ships this.
- Decision: Partly restored. `UI.environment` and `Facet.adaptive` publish the viewport classes, orientation, interaction classes, display size, safe insets, preferred text size and reduced motion again (`mech1-02`, `mech3-11`). [Restored before the merge](#restored-before-the-merge) names the tests. The preview profiles, `selectBy` and the distance profile stay retired. Native layout replaces the `ViewThatFits` choice: `AbsoluteSize`, `AutomaticSize` and `UIListLayout.Wraps`.

### 8. Presenter toasts

- Main promised: `presentToast` queued, stacked, timed and dismissed toasts in a display band, with reduced-motion transitions and HUD reservations.
- Main evidence: 5 contracts, 34 main cases: `mech2-82` (2, class c, `native_toast`); `mech3-86` (13, class c, `toast_layer_seam`); `mech3-88` (13, class c, `toast_presentation`); `mech3-90` (1, class c, `toast_schedule`); `apps-220` (5, class c, `hud_reservations`).
- Strengthening notes that retire part of a covered contract: `mech3-87`, `mech3-89`, `apps2-226`, `mech2-12`.
- PR status: The toast is now a gallery scenario (`examples/gallery/scenarios/sponsor_toast.luau`). Each game writes its own queue and timers.
- Current main `bc9a56a6` still ships this.
- Decision: Restored by another change. The overlays port restores toasts.

### 9. Custom and anchored presentation

- Main promised: `app.presentModal` showed any component as a modal with a scrim and `outsideTapCancel`, and `presentAnchored` placed a surface at a source path or rectangle.
- Main evidence: 4 contracts, 41 main cases: `navigation-1-46` (7, class c, `anchored_surface`); `mech2-105` (21, class c, `presenter`); `mech1-84` (11, class c, `compose_scene`); `navigation-2-62` (2, class c, `modal_dismissal`).
- PR status: `UI.Alert` and `UI.Sheet` own presentation. A game that needs a custom modal body must fit it in a Sheet or build it on native objects.
- Current main `bc9a56a6` still ships this.
- Decision: Restored by another change. The overlays port restores custom and anchored presentation.

### 10. Server replication helpers

- Main promised: `Facet.replication` kept revisioned snapshots and collections, repaired gaps and ran optimistic mutations with confirm, reject and reset.
- Main evidence: 3 contracts, 24 main cases: `apps2-81` (17, class c, `replication`, `fuzz_replication`); `apps2-162` (6, class c, `platform_adapters_recovery`); `apps2-223` (1, class c, `replication`).
- PR status: `docs/guide/06-client-server.md` teaches a hand-written request model. The settings-sync example writes its own loopback.
- Current main `bc9a56a6` still ships this.
- Decision: Accepted. Server state is game domain. [Client and server](06-client-server.md) shows the request model.

### 11. Generic drag and drop

- Main promised: `UI.draggable` and `UI.dropTarget` gave hover, legality, drop, cancel, gamepad pickup and a drag proxy for any control.
- Main evidence: 3 contracts, 81 main cases: `inputs-84` (53, class c, `drag_public`); `inputs-85` (11, class c, `drag_session`); `apps2-246` (17, class c, `sponsor_scenarios`).
- PR status: Only table and list reorder keep a drag. A game uses `UIDragDetector` directly (the match-3 example does).
- Current main `bc9a56a6` still ships this.
- Decision: Accepted. A game uses the native `UIDragDetector`.

### 12. On-screen keyboard avoidance

- Main promised: A focused TextInput moved up out from under the on-screen keyboard, and `keyboardType` and `submitLabel` mapped to the keyboard.
- Main evidence: 3 contracts, 16 main cases: `apps-244` (1, class c, `adaptive_defaults`); `inputs-08` (1, class c, `auto_input`); `inputs-189` (14, class c, `text_input`).
- Strengthening notes that retire part of a covered contract: `inputs-10`.
- PR status: The PR leaves this to the engine TextBox. No test or live record shows that the engine keeps a field visible on a phone.
- Current main `bc9a56a6` still ships this.
- Decision: Accepted. The engine TextBox owns the on-screen keyboard.

### 13. Haptic and sensory feedback system

- Main promised: A twelve-verb feedback taxonomy, `UI.sensoryFeedback`, a feedback bus and a haptics adapter with rate limits and profiles.
- Main evidence: 4 contracts, 209 main cases: `inputs-75` (64, class c, `control_feedback`); `inputs-89` (104, class c, `haptics`); `inputs-169` (14, class c, `sensory_feedback`); `inputs-170` (27, class c, `sensory_profile`).
- Strengthening notes that retire part of a covered contract: `inputs-76`.
- PR status: The PR keeps one `pressHaptic` option that plays only on a value change. The motor output on a phone and a gamepad has no physical evidence (`tools/lune/parity_blockers.json` `pendingLiveRisks`).
- Current main `bc9a56a6` still ships this.
- Decision: Accepted. A game plays a native `HapticEffect`. The controls play the factory `pressHaptic` option for a change of state or value.

### 14. Table selection and editing model

- Main promised: A click moved keyboard focus, a plain click replaced a multi selection, Ctrl-click toggled, Ctrl or Shift with an arrow extended the selection, a double click ran `onPrimaryAction`, and the table owned its Edit/Done toggle.
- Main evidence: 7 contracts, 35 main cases: `collections-148` (2, class c, `table`); `collections-150` (2, class c, `table`); `collections-198` (4, class c, `table_input`); `collections-185` (19, class c, `table_input`); `collections-105` (1, class c, `row_actions_scenario`); `collections-41` (3, class c, `paradigm_table`); `collections-38` (4, class c, `paradigm_table`).
- Strengthening notes that retire part of a covered contract: `apps2-179`.
- PR status: In multi mode every activation toggles. Arrows do not select. The caller owns `editing`. Desktop players lose the usual list selection keys.
- Current main `bc9a56a6` still ships this.
- Decision: Restored in part. The multi-select keys are restored: a plain click replaces, Ctrl-click or Cmd-click toggles, Shift-click and Shift with an arrow extend. A plain arrow still does not select. The decision does not cover `onPrimaryAction` and the table-owned Edit and Done toggle. They stay retired.

### 15. Row tray dismissal

- Main promised: A tap outside an open row tray closed it without swallowing the tap.
- Main evidence: 3 contracts, 13 main cases: `collections-78` (1, class c, `row_actions_input`); `collections-90` (2, class c, `row_actions_input`); `collections-285` (10, class c, `virtual_list_row_actions`).
- PR status: The PR RowActions has no outside catcher. A tray stays open until the row closes it.
- Current main `bc9a56a6` still ships this.
- Decision: Restored.

### 16. Error boundary control

- Main promised: `UI.ErrorBoundary` contained a failing region, showed a fallback and retried a failing row factory.
- Main evidence: 1 contracts, 8 main cases: `mech1-115` (8, class c, `error_boundary`).
- PR status: The PR contains failures only for Alert content and NavigationStack destinations. Main `bc9a56a6` extended this control (commit `e09f648c`, `G1`), so the port to main must decide.
- Current main `bc9a56a6` still ships this.
- Decision: Restored. The presenter critical screen stays retired.

### 17. Declarative motion API

- Main promised: `UI.withAnimation`, region `transition` on `UI.When` and `UI.ForEach`, the marquee reveal of truncated text, the typewriter stream and the value count-up.
- Main evidence: 5 contracts, 68 main cases: `mech3-99` (29, class c, `with_animation`); `mech1-105` (10, class c, `declarative_animation`); `mech3-78` (14, class c, `text_reveal`); `mech3-79` (5, class c, `text_reveal_stream`); `mech3-96` (10, class c, `value_reveal`).
- Strengthening notes that retire part of a covered contract: `apps2-155`, `mech3-98`.
- PR status: Authors compose `runtime.spring` and `runtime.tween`. Controls animate by default since `4b9ab031`. AGENTS.md on main still teaches `transition` on `UI.When` and `UI.ForEach`.
- Current main `bc9a56a6` still ships this.
- Decision: Accepted. Controls animate by default, and a game uses Compose animation (`runtime.spring`, `runtime.tween`) for its own motion.

### 18. Text fit and middle truncation

- Main promised: `textSize = "fit"` chose the largest size that fits, and long values could truncate in the middle.
- Main evidence: 2 contracts, 32 main cases: `mech3-83` (18, class c, `text_size_fit`); `mech2-46` (14, class c, `leaf_capabilities`).
- PR status: Only native end truncation and `TextScaled` remain.
- Current main `bc9a56a6` still ships this.
- Decision: Restored.

### 19. Programmatic scrolling and reorder autoscroll

- Main promised: `scrollTo`, `scrollToVisible`, the shared keep-visible clamp, and autoscroll with dwell, bands and a nested chain during a reorder.
- Main evidence: 4 contracts, 44 main cases: `apps-228` (20, class c, `native_scroll`); `collections-01` (10, class c, `autoscroll`); `collections-02` (7, class c, `autoscroll`); `collections-181` (7, class c, `table_input`).
- Strengthening notes that retire part of a covered contract: `mech1-42`.
- PR status: VirtualList keeps `indexOfKey`, `placementOf` and `offsetOf`. Reorder autoscroll starts at once and scrolls only its own body.
- Current main `bc9a56a6` still ships this.
- Decision: Partly restored. `UI.scrollTo` and `UI.scrollToVisible` move native `ScrollingFrame` ancestors (`apps-228`). [Restored before the merge](#restored-before-the-merge) names the tests. The autoscroll dwell, bands and nested chain stay retired.

### 20. World anchor projection

- Main promised: `client.world_anchor` projected a world object to a screen anchor with padding, tracking and near-plane rejection.
- Main evidence: 1 contracts, 6 main cases: `apps-223` (6, class c, `world_anchor`).
- PR status: `docs/guide/14-choosing-controls.md` tells the game to project with the camera. AGENTS.md on main still names `client.world_anchor`.
- Current main `bc9a56a6` still ships this.
- Decision: Restored. `UI.worldAnchor` projects a world object to a screen anchor again (`apps-223`). [Restored before the merge](#restored-before-the-merge) names the tests.

### 21. Engine-owned mechanisms with no proof

- Main promised: 47 class b contracts (199 main cases) that now depend on an engine or Compose mechanism and have no candidate test or live record.
- Evidence: their `nativeEvidence` is `none`. No candidate test and no live record shows that Facet uses the mechanism correctly. Examples: `inputs-79` (D-pad and thumbstick repeat cadence), `inputs-134` (thumbstick latch and dead zone), `mech2-35` (no focus ring after touch). The class b tests in [Engine mechanism tests](20-verification-parity.md#engine-mechanism-tests) closed 81 more of these contracts.
- Decision: accept these on the engine's word, or ask for live checks (`docs/guide/11-device-verification.md` harness).

### 22. Gamepad haptic motor output

- Main promised: a physical gamepad feels the Button, selection and custom haptic pulses.
- Evidence: the haptic request is proven headless (`native_inputs`), and a physical phone felt the press haptic in the published showcase.
- Decision: engine-owned. The Roblox `HapticEffect` and `PressHapticEffect` drive the motor. `tools/lune/parity_blockers.json` records it under `engineOwnedLiveRisks`.

### 23. Vector paths

- Main promised: `UI.Path` strokes normalized points on a native `Path2D`, and `Facet.pathShapes` makes arcs, rings and needles.
- Decision: Restored. `UI.Path` takes `points`, `role` or `tint`, `thickness` in pixels or a metric name, and `closed`. `Facet.pathShapes` has `arc`, `ring`, `needle` and `MAX_CONTROL_POINTS`. Tests: `native_parity_restore` (Path) and the live case `paint_live/path-strokes-in-theme-roles` in nine packages.

### 24. Grid column flow

- Main promised: a grid that fills each column before the next.
- Decision: Restored. `UI.Grid { flow = "column" }` fills columns first and sets its rows from the number of children. Test: `native_layout` (Grid).

### 25. Content-based heights

- Main promised: a height given in lines, rows, another node (`of`) or a type role.
- Decision: Restored in part. `UI.Text { lines = n }` shows at most `n` lines and ends with an ellipsis. The `rows`, `of` and `role` height forms are accepted as retired: a game sets a pixel height, `AutomaticSize` or a `UISizeConstraint`. Test: `native_parity_restore` (Label text fit and middle truncation).

### 26. Rich text escaping

- Main promised: `Facet.richText.escape` for player names and server strings in rich text.
- Decision: Restored. Tests: `native_parity_restore` (Label text fit and middle truncation).

### 27. Layout arrangements

- Main promised: `UI.Composition` and `UI.Region` arrangements, spans and step-down, and `UI.AdaptiveStack`, which turned its axis by space.
- Decision: Accepted. Native layout does this: a `UIListLayout` with `Wraps`, a `FillDirection` bound to `UI.environment`, and `UIFlexItem`. [Adaptive recipes](15-adaptive-recipes.md) shows the patterns.

### 28. Margins, size caps and aspect with fill

- Main promised: outer margins, minimum and maximum sizes, and an aspect ratio on a filling child.
- Decision: Accepted. A game uses the container `gap` and `padding`, a native `UISizeConstraint`, and a native `UIAspectRatioConstraint`.

### 29. Conditional and list helpers

- Main promised: `UI.When`, `UI.ForEach` and `UI.ForPairs` with an enter and exit `transition`, and a shared element named by `transition.source`.
- Decision: Accepted. `Compose.show`, `Compose.keyed` and `Compose.presence` do this. `Compose.presence` gives a region an `exiting` phase and holds its removal until an exit animation completes. The shared-element transition is not replaced.

### 30. Foreign hosts and spec types

- Main promised: `UI.Foreign` adopted an existing instance, and `ButtonSpec` and `ScrollViewSpec` named option tables.
- Decision: Accepted. A game makes the instance with a Host constructor and releases it with `Compose.cleanup`. The option types are `ButtonProps` and the layout props.

### 31. Full text of a truncated title

- Main promised: a truncated title showed its full value on request.
- Decision: Accepted. A title truncates with the native `TextTruncate.AtEnd`. A game that needs the full value shows it in a Callout or a detail view.

### 32. Imperative Callout and path sources

- Main promised: `api.dismiss`, `api.rearm` and `api.present` on a Callout, and a Popover source addressed by a path.
- Decision: Accepted. A Callout follows its caller's facts, and a new generation is a new keyed control. A Popover source is one Instance.

### 33. Paint differences in the native StyleSheet

- Main promised: three see-through backgrounds in a compiled sheet, a surface scrim, a default utility outline, a separate count pill on a text tab, and related paint details.
- Decision: Accepted. The native StyleSheet paints each state with its own rule, and `api.md` documents the native paint.

### 34. Main-internal assertions and device-only checks

- Main promised: assertions on machinery that the cutover deleted, for example text premeasurement and calibration, the solver's exact rectangles, the gesture arbiter and the upload count of a path.
- Decision: Accepted. `api.md` does not promise this machinery. The candidate proves the visible result. Contracts that need a physical device or an input that Studio cannot send (multi-touch, the on-screen keyboard, a motor, Tab and DPadUp) are checked on a device. Each contract has a `closeNote` in `tools/lune/verification_parity.json`.

### 35. Minimum touch targets

- Main promised: a compact or xsmall control keeps a 44 pixel hit footprint for touch.
- Decision: Restored for touch and gamepad devices. A Button with a `controlSize` below the minimum target grows to `targetSizes.minimum` on both axes when the device has touch or a gamepad. The native button is its hit area, so the plate grows with the target; main painted a smaller plate inside a larger footprint. On a mouse-only device the compact size stays. Test: `native_inputs` (floors a compact Button to the minimum touch target).

## Restored before the merge

These contracts moved from class c to class a. The cases are in `tests/native_parity_restore.spec.luau`. Each contract in `tools/lune/verification_parity.json` has `restoredBy` set to `parity/restore`.

| Contract | Main cases | Restored behavior | Native implementation | Still weaker |
|---|---:|---|---|---|
| `mech1-115` | 8 | `UI.ErrorBoundary` contains a failure, shows its fallback, reports the failure once and can retry. | `Compose.boundary` | The presenter critical screen is not restored. |
| `collections-150` | 2 | A plain mouse click replaces a multi selection. Ctrl-click or Cmd-click toggles a row. | `UserInputService:IsKeyDown` and the `Activated` input object | - |
| `collections-198` | 4 | Shift with an arrow key, Home or End extends the selection from the anchor. Ctrl with an arrow moves the focus only. | The collection arrow actions and the Compose focus scope | A plain arrow does not replace the selection. |
| `collections-78` | 1 | A tap outside an open tray closes it and is not swallowed. | `UserInputService.InputBegan`, observed only while the tray is open | - |
| `collections-90` | 2 | An outside tap during a destructive commit does not stop the commit. | The same observer | There is no passive HUD catcher. |
| `collections-285` | 10 | A press on another row closes the open row. | The same observer | A press on the content of the open row keeps its tray open. There is no shared list overlay. |
| `mech3-83` | 18 | Text `textSize = "fit"` paints the largest size that fits, between a cap and a floor. | `TextScaled` and `UITextSizeConstraint` | The engine picks the size. The headless engine does not scale text. |
| `mech2-46` | 14 | Text `truncate = "middle"` keeps the start and the end of a long value. | The engine `TextBounds` of the label | The headless engine measures with a fixed glyph width. |

The focus rules change moved three more contracts to class a. The cases are in `tests/native_selection_parity.spec.luau`. Each contract has `restoredBy` set to `parity/gamepad`. The live routes are in `artifacts/gamepad/gamepad-parity-live.json`.

| Contract | Main cases | Restored behavior | Native implementation | Still weaker |
|---|---:|---|---|---|
| `apps-158` | 15 | Tab and Shift+Tab walk the controls in layout order, wrap, skip hidden and disabled controls and stay inside a modal. | A Facet `InputAction` on Tab that sets `GuiService.SelectedObject` | Studio cannot inject Tab. The live check fires the action. There are no main navigation groups. |
| `collections-260` | 17 | A removed focused row gives the selection to the next row, or to the previous row at the end. | The Compose focus scope and `GuiService.SelectedObject` | The `focusPolicy` field and its index policy stay removed. |
| `collections-306` | 1 | The same nearest-survivor rule for a virtualized list. | The same | - |

Facet Neutral's `Light` palette (main `b1a08042`) is newer than the baseline. Its contract `post-lab-02` moves from class d to class a. Facet Neutral declares `Dark` and `Light` again. Both palettes pass the contrast gate, and the theme strength specs check both palettes where they checked one before.

The environment, world anchor and scrolling change moved four more contracts to class a. The cases are in `tests/native_environment.spec.luau`. Each contract has `restoredBy` set to `parity/restore3`.

| Contract | Main cases | Restored behavior | Native implementation | Still weaker |
|---|---:|---|---|---|
| `mech1-02` | 36 | `Facet.adaptive` classes, axis and columns. `UI.environment` size class, height class, orientation and boolean tests that follow the viewport. | `AbsoluteSize` or `Camera.ViewportSize`, Compose formulas | The ten-foot cap, `navPlacement` and the four navigation homes stay retired. Controls do not read the environment. |
| `mech3-11` | 7 | Interaction classes, display size, safe insets, preferred text size and reduced motion as separate readables. | `UserInputService.PreferredInput`, `GuiService:GetGuiInset()`, `PreferredTextSize`, `ReducedMotionEnabled` | Keyboard occlusion, overscan and distance facts stay retired. |
| `apps-223` | 6 | `UI.worldAnchor` projection, padding, offscreen directions, occlusion, tracking and near-plane rejection. | `Camera:WorldToViewportPoint`, `Workspace:Raycast`, `RunService.Heartbeat` | - |
| `apps-228` | 20 | `UI.scrollTo` and `UI.scrollToVisible` with the minimum keep-visible distance, inner-to-outer ancestors and clamping. | `ScrollingFrame.CanvasPosition`, `TweenService` | There is no path controller, `observeScroll` or scroll-bar inset. |

The controls restore moved two more contracts to class a. The cases are in `tests/native_restored_controls.spec.luau`. Each contract has `restoredBy` set to `parity/controls-restore`. The same change restored `UI.Text`, main's `UI.Label` (an icon and a title), `UI.Image`, `UI.Divider` and `UI.Spacer`. Contracts that tested a part of these controls that stays deleted, such as Text `disclose`, `over` and `surface`, Image `imageFraming` and baseline alignment, stay in class c. Their lines below say what is restored.

| Contract | Main cases | Restored behavior | Native implementation | Still weaker |
|---|---:|---|---|---|
| `mech1-106` | 5 | A divider spends the hairline opacity of the theme in every shipped package, is quieter than secondary content, and a strong divider is heavier. | The `facet-divider` and `facet-divider-strong` StyleSheet rules | The non-native adapter path is deleted. |
| `themes-P1-57` | 2 | A circle Image refuses a nine-slice mode, and image framing is refused. | `UICorner` and `UIAspectRatioConstraint` on a native ImageLabel | `imageFraming` is not restored, so its refusal is the unknown-option error. |

The menu parity change moved three more contracts to class a. The cases are in `tests/native_menu_parity.spec.luau`. Each contract has `restoredBy` set to `parity/menu`. The live evidence is in `artifacts/captures/menu/menu-live.json`.

| Contract | Main cases | Restored behavior | Native implementation | Still weaker |
|---|---:|---|---|---|
| `navigation-2-48` | 6 | The floating panel is one raised card with flat rows, one hairline between each pair of rows and an accent fill under an `onAccent` label on a selected row. The sheet keeps its rows. | The `facet-menu`, `facet-menu-group`, `facet-menu-hairline` and `facet-menu-item.facet-selected` StyleSheet rules, `UICorner`, `UIStroke` and `UIShadow` | A list long enough to scroll rounds the ends of its content, not the ends of the viewport. |
| `navigation-2-49` | 3 | Each level scales from 0.96 and fades in from the corner where it hangs, and dips out faster. A sheet submenu slides forward on entry and backward on Back. Reduced motion removes the travel. | `UIScale`, `CanvasGroup.GroupTransparency` and a Compose timeline | - |
| `paint-61` | 1 | `trigger` attaches the menu to any node and returns that node. `label` and `trigger` together are refused. | Native events and input actions on the trigger node | - |

Three main controls are not restored:

- `UI.PopupButton` was deprecated on main. Use `UI.Picker { style = "menu", options = options, selected = cell }`.
- `UI.TextField` was the renderer leaf under TextInput on main. Use `UI.TextInput { value = cell }`.
- `UI.Gauge` was the name of a theme example on main, not a control. Use `UI.ProgressView { presentation = "circular" }` or a custom control (`examples/themes/custom_control.luau`).

## Retired contracts by area

Each line gives the contract id, the main case count and the main specs, the promise, the reason for the retirement and the replacement test. "Engine or Compose owns it" names the native mechanism. "Deleted" gives the audit evidence from `api.md`, the guides or `src`.

### Reactive core

2 class b and 6 class c contracts, 40 main cases.

- `mech1-137` (3 main cases; `fuzz_scheduler`). Main promised: Seeded scheduler fuzz invariants and replay. Engine or Compose owns it: Compose scheduler (vendored). Replacement test: yes: native_stress runs 400 replayable seeds over keyed collections, not scheduler-level invariants..
- `mech1-65` (1 main case; `compose_backend`). Main promised: Delivery order across separately created memos. Engine or Compose owns it: Compose scheduler (vendored src/vendor/compose). Replacement test: no test (none in Facet tests; relies on upstream Compose.).
- `mech1-122` (1 main case; `faults`). Main promised: Scheduler feedback converges or hits the cap loudly. Deleted: Facet scheduler feedback/settle cap deleted.. Replacement test: none; the feature is deleted.
- `mech1-70` (5 main cases; `compose_contract`). Main promised: Settle pass convergence, 100-round quarantine. Deleted: src/render/settle_pass.luau and services error boundary deleted; no settle loop exists (guide 15-adaptive-recipes: 'Do not run a second settle loop').. Replacement test: none; the feature is deleted.
- `mech1-87` (3 main cases; `conformance/conformance`). Main promised: Micro-screen conformance fixtures. Deleted: Conformance fixture harness and dumps deleted.. Replacement test: none; the feature is deleted.
- `mech2-113` (11 main cases; `reactive_recovery`). Main promised: Settle quarantine, drain cap, lone-observer snapshot guarantees. Deleted: Facet settle pass deleted; observer/drain semantics belong to vendored Compose (tested upstream, not in CAND). Replacement test: none; the feature is deleted.
- `mech3-38` (14 main cases; `settle_containment`). Main promised: Settle pass once per flush, vendored hook assertions, failure announcement throttling. Deleted: src/core/services settle pass and core.lastError deleted; api.md 'There is no ... reactor'. Replacement test: none; the feature is deleted.
- `themes-P2-61` (2 main cases; `theme_icons_applied`). Main promised: Adapter sequencing: the icon seam runs before early returns, and the snapshot arrives inside the swap transaction. Deleted: screen_chrome, screen_target and theme_controller are removed. Reactive swaps are covered by Compose cells, as in the icon swap in 'keeps native semantic icons reactive...'.. Replacement test: none; the feature is deleted.

### Text measurement and fit

30 class b and 20 class c contracts, 402 main cases.

- `apps-130` (1 main case; `semantic_rows`). Main promised: Described toggle fits beside natural-width value in a narrow theme. Engine or Compose owns it: Native TextButton text layout with UIPadding reserved for the indicator. Replacement test: yes: native_inputs::native input controls::uses native Toggle text and reserved padding while retaining rich hints and live theme insets (asserts padding offsets, not narrow fit).
- `apps-248` (4 main cases; `large_text_hot_swap`). Main promised: Geometry returns byte-identical and files no overlap/clip finding. Engine or Compose owns it: Engine text layout (TextSize/AutomaticSize/TextWrapped); solver findings deleted. Replacement test: no test.
- `apps-249` (3 main cases; `large_text_layout`). Main promised: Form in a scroll view reflows then scrolls; content grows while viewport does not; layout identical under reduced motion. Engine or Compose owns it: ScrollingFrame AutomaticCanvasSize + AutomaticSize/TextWrapped. Replacement test: live Studio evidence `classb_keyboard/form-reflows-then-scrolls`, `classb_keyboard/form-geometry-identical-under-reduced-motion`.
- `apps-251` (68 main cases; `large_text_matrix`). Main promised: Every control at four text preferences x two themes has no overlap, undisclosed clip or sub-floor target. Engine or Compose owns it: Engine text layout (TextSize, TextWrapped, AutomaticSize, UITextSizeConstraint) in native controls. Replacement test: no test (none (no candidate sweep over controls x preferences; native_text_preference::native text preference updates::keeps editing focus row identity and scroll position across text and theme changes covers three controls)).
- `apps-255` (5 main cases; `large_text_matrix`). Main promised: Action labels wrap before truncating; fixed px widths clip; mixed CJK/emoji/latin lines grow monotonically. Engine or Compose owns it: Engine TextWrapped/AutomaticSize and text shaping. Replacement test: live Studio evidence `classb_keyboard/action-label-wraps-before-truncating`, `classb_keyboard/fixed-px-action-draws-compact-form`, `classb_keyboard/mixed-script-lines-grow-monotonically`.

## Parts of covered contracts that are retired

These contracts are class a: a candidate test covers them. While the tests were made stronger, one part of each promise was found to be no longer promised. The note gives the evidence. The source is the result file in the parity work folder.

- `apps-125` (Loads headless and a public-API slice mounts, binds and tears down). Status after strengthening: full. Note: VERSION equals the newest changelog entry; Button and Label slice binds, activates and tears down to the census baseline. The Phase-1 Facet.new API is retired. Source: `weakC/surface.result.json`.
- `apps-131` (Mount/unmount laps, scrolled pooled surface and ramp laps return instance count to baseline). Status after strengthening: partial. Note: Three full ramp laps after warm-up return instances, connections and owners to the floor, and teardown returns to baseline. Retired: fake_target parked-handle pool (no recycle pool in the native renderer). The ramp laps use a short ladder of 40, 80 and 120 rows. The declared ceiling rungs (10000 and 40000 rows) take more than a minute headless, so the suite does not run them. Source: `weakC/lifetime.result.json`.
- `apps-140` (Every composite's ref identifies its root once (34 controls)). Status after strengthening: full. Note: native_conformance now also asserts that ref runs exactly once per build and not again on a property update, for every public control. The frozen-record shape is retired: ref receives the native root. Source: `weakC/surface.result.json`.
- `apps-147` (Removing the focused item falls to the nearest surviving neighbor). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Nearest-survivor fallback after removing a general focused node is no longer a live promise. docs/guide/07-input.md:36-39 promises only restoration when the requested collection row exists. docs/reference/api.md:224 promises removeFocusFallback only for Chip, and the candidate covers it. The Compose focus scope clears a removed key (src/vendor/compose/core/focus-scope.luau, the source watch). The new case proves that a removed focused row never leaves selection stranded on a destroyed node. Source: `weakB/result-focus-selection.json`.
- `apps-159` (Entry focus shown for controller, not painted for mouse/touch). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Screen-level entry focus from a presenter is retired: the native GuiService owns screen selection, and docs/guide/07-input.md:17-24 promises entry selection only for modal controls. The new case extends the modal-entry proof to Sheet and Menu for Touch, KeyboardAndMouse and Gamepad. Source: `weakB/result-focus-selection.json`.
- `apps-167` (Stepper is driven by Activate and the Adjust keys). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Space/Return Activate on the selected Decrease/Increase button steps both ways; Period/Comma and bumpers step from a descendant or the root; no Adjust action while focus is outside. Main's claim that arrows only move between the step buttons is retired: the branch binds Left/Right as bounded adjust actions that yield to navigation at the limits (src/ui/inputs.luau adjustment()); api.md says only that keyboard and gamepad adjustment use the input actions of the control. Source: `weakB/result-controls-values.json`.
- `apps-185` (Replacing order keeps focus or lands on nearest survivor). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Focus stays on its key through a VirtualList reorder and replacement and through a Table sort change, and the next Down moves from the new position. For a removed TabView tab, the survivor case already exists in native_navigation. Nearest survivor for collection rows is not promised (see apps-147). Source: `weakB/result-focus-selection.json`.
- `apps-190` (Phone gamepad keeps compact flow through input switches; ten-foot larger geometry). Status after strengthening: full. Note: The input-class switch is asserted. The ten-foot distanceProfile environment fact is retired (no src or api.md reference). Ten-foot geometry is now the gallery viewing-distance UIScale 1.5, which the gallery preview case and native_gallery_shell assert. Source: `weakB/result-navigation-containers.json`.
- `apps-195` (iconOnly renders glyph and refuses incompatible options). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Glyph equals themes.resolveIcon(checkmark), title text empty, FacetLabel kept; absent icon refused for segmented, inline, radioGroup and menu, static and live, and a live replacement that drops the icon errors. Refusing radio + iconOnly is retired: api.md:279 lists iconOnly as a plain Picker option and src/ui/nav_menu.luau:754 only requires an icon on each option. Source: `weakB/result-menus-pickers.json`.
- `apps2-11` (Reset mid-cascade stops the machine and leaves no retiring tile; dispose mid-cascade releases the hook). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Reset mid-cascade: phase idle, empty log, not resolving, no tick hook, score/moves 0, retiring tile destroyed after settle, only live tile ids mounted, machine still plays; dispose mid-swap drops the hook; full census baseline. Source: `weakC/lifetime.result.json`.
- `apps2-129` (Every exit reason lands in the same state; exited terminal sends nothing and re-engages; no transport is a state). Status after strengthening: full. Note: All eight exit reasons from a pending request: exited, reason recorded, request cancelled, onExit once, second exit refused, stale confirm ignored; 20 engage/cancel cycles then send; no-transport gives status rejected (bug fixed). Retired: exit result table {reason, already} is now a boolean. Source: `weakC/lifetime.result.json`.
- `apps2-134` (Console ten-foot profile applies ten-foot facts to the environment). Status after strengthening: full. Note: The profile facts are applied to the native PreferredInput and the ten-foot gamepad policies engage (44 px table rows, inline automatic picker) and revert with desktop facts. The derived type floor (typographyScale 1.5) and overscan are retired with src/env: Facet has no environment module and src/ui has no ten-foot, overscan or text-scale derivation; the engine owns overscan and PreferredTextSize. Source: `weakB/result-adaptive-environment.json`.
- `apps2-145` (Run all sweeps every workload, stop halts between workloads, ramp climbs and stops, reset returns to idle). Status after strengthening: full. Note: An injected failing workload does not end Run all; the final line names the capture count and the JSON output; stop halts between workloads; reset destroys the workload node. The main 'says where it stopped' text is retired: the lab has no such message. Source: `weakC/textperf.result.json`.
- `apps2-155` (Motion-flight pass profiles an animation flight and cleans up). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Motion-flight moves all 24 springs every frame, reports 30 control samples in a valid capture, the idle control reports none, and the next selection destroys the chips and returns heartbeat listeners to baseline. Retired: withAnimation position/size/paint arms no longer exist. Source: `weakC/motion.result.json`.
- `apps2-163` (PreloadAsync receives Instances). Status after strengthening: full. Note: PreloadAsync gets a one-item list holding the retained ImageLabel (an Instance, Image = key); no instance is made or destroyed during preload; ready and failed paths; retired: the temporary per-key target and its destroy, because the ImageLabel is now retained. Source: `weakC/icons.result.json`.
- `apps2-179` (A selected column is released on Cancel, second Activate, or when focus leaves the table). Status after strengthening: full. Source: `weakC/icons.result.json`.
- `themes-P1-21` (Presence mark uses a shared status cutout). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Presence child of the Avatar has the Cutout inner border stroke (tag, thickness 1) and sits in the bottom-right corner at 12px; retired: the separate cutout rect geometry (the cutout is now a stroke). Source: `weakC/icons.result.json`.
- `themes-P1-33` (Refuses bad members and malformed container policies). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Fifteen malformed specs refused (missing or false items, non-dense, empty name or id, bad presence, max 0/1.5/huge, grid, bad overflow, bad form, onOverflow=true, huge rung, unknown option); empty group has no faces or chip; fixed missing form and onOverflow validation; retired: member key field (provider API deleted). Source: `weakC/icons.result.json`.
- `themes-P1-39` (Bound-nil presence survives source replacement). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Keyed face keeps identity through nil -> online -> refused invalid -> nil -> image replacement -> busy; label and mark follow; chip activates; empty roster destroys the face; retired: per-member presenceLabel (not in AvatarMember). Source: `weakC/icons.result.json`.
- `themes-P1-44` (Distinct identities; repeated user ids share one request). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Distinct images keep distinct ImageLabels; repeated userId with one caller shared resource loads once and releases when both leave; image change keeps the keyed face; userId gives the rbxthumb URL; retired: automatic dedupe without a caller resource. Source: `weakC/icons.result.json`.
- `themes-P1-46` (Neutral plate with no focus stop). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Resolved control fill, opaque plate, control corner, padding and content text/icon in every shipped palette, no status tag, nothing selectable. Retired: an explicit neutral status now uses the shared neutral status pair (asserted). Source: `weakC/paint.result.json`.
- `themes-P1-51` (Requires meaningful content and icon-only name). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Fixed: an invalid iconPosition was silently leading (see bugs). Main's refusal of iconPosition without an icon and of a raw asset id as icon are not promised by api.md. Source: `weakB/result-controls-values.json`.
- `themes-P1-65` (Indeterminate head travels at fixed sweep). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Head travels while the sweep stays exactly 90 degrees on every frame. Retired: 'no backing ring' (api.md is silent; RingTrack stays). Source: `weakC/motion.result.json`.
- `themes-P1-77` (Indeterminate view reports animating under reduced motion). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Under reduced motion the cycle keeps a heartbeat subscription, the readout says Loading and the indicator steps. Retired: record.dump().animating. Source: `weakC/motion.result.json`.
- `themes-P2-06` (A nine-slice skin paints the package's own image values). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Parchment panel skin: exact Image, ScaleType Slice, SliceCenter Rect(40,40,104,104), SliceScale; the art has no tags so no StyleRule can tint it, ImageColor3 white and ImageTransparency 0; target skinned; retired: the $ChromeTint sheet token. Source: `weakC/icons.result.json`.
- `themes-P2-40` (Semantic icon map with state variants and namespaced custom names resolves per state). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Undeclared pressed/selected/disabled fall back to default for a framework and a namespaced name, including on a mounted Button; retired: the compiled iconArt sibling (resolveIcon reads the map directly). Source: `weakC/icons.result.json`.
- `themes-P2-44` (The settings gear resolves to the archived asset). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Exact rbxassetid://78513807849201 from an empty registry, the neutral package, a defined package, the ART row, and a mounted Picker LeadingIcon; retired: the ASCII fallback glyph. Source: `weakC/icons.result.json`.
- `themes-P2-49` (Pixel Quest keeps its own pixel art and fills missing action icons from the framework). Status after strengthening: full. Note: menu, flag, facet:search and close resolve; close is the package cross, not framework art; unmapped trash is framework art; retired: ASCII glyph floor for buildWithoutIcons (it now resolves framework art). Source: `weakC/icons.result.json`.
- `themes-P2-50` (Shipped themes draw the real magnifying glass, and package art overrides it). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Exact rbxassetid://85188541706347 on neutral, pixel_quest, compact_pointer and glossy_touch; package override reaches a mounted icon; retired: the ASCII floor. Source: `weakC/icons.result.json`.
- `themes-P2-60` (The icon element resolves, sizes, tags and pixel-samples, and dies with its owner). Status after strengthening: full. Note: ctx.icon resolves and re-resolves, 20x20, transparent, facet-icon tag, Active false, Default then Pixelated resampling, unknown name refused, destroyed on stop and census back to baseline; retired: the ICON_Z_INDEX ladder. Source: `weakC/icons.result.json`.
- `themes-P2-66` (Changing icon content re-renders mounted icons). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Zero Image writes on palette and map-order changes; content change, state-map change and declaration removal re-render the same instances; retired: asset tintRole (paint uses the .facet-icon rule). Source: `weakC/icons.result.json`.
- `themes-P3-01` (Package compiles to a caller-linked native StyleSheet; teardown destroys its rules). Status after strengthening: full. Note: One caller-parented named sheet and exactly one StyleLink referencing it; controls read the same package metrics; stop destroys sheet, every rule, link and button with an instance/connection census back to the pre-install level, while a pre-existing sheet and link stay intact and linked. Retired: theme_controller install/uninstall API. Source: `weakC/themepkg.result.json`.
- `themes-P3-31` (Default/compiled theme data is immutable and cannot be corrupted by callers). Status after strengthening: full. Note: neutralPackage copies are independent at every level; define output is recursively frozen, writes throw, later definition edits do not leak, and a derived package is frozen too. Retired: the shared frozen neutral() snapshot; api.md says neutralPackage returns a mutable copy. Source: `weakC/themepkg.result.json`.
- `themes-P3-33` (Named palette selection). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Selects each declared palette by name on one reactive sheet (3 two-palette packages), the default when unnamed, keeps rule identity. Retired: rejecting an undeclared name; api.md does not promise it, and the sheet falls back to a declared palette (asserted). Source: `weakC/themepkg.result.json`.
- `themes-P4-01` (Layer box geometry: full-bleed fill/frame/tile, four corner ornaments, per-side edge rails, plaque overhang and text insets). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Pins every box: full-bleed fill/frame/tile, four anchored 24px corners with per-corner art, top/bottom and left/right rails stretching on the other axis, plaques overhanging on all four edges, plaque caption padding from textInsets, ZIndex and array order. Retired: canonical edge order; slots are now retained and named per side. Source: `weakC/themepkg.result.json`.
- `themes-P4-16` (A layered recipe using the full vocabulary compiles and renders). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Fantasy Ornate's six-kind panel renders 10 images with each layer's asset, scale type, slice centre, tile size, rail box, distinct corner art, plaque box and caption padding, plus its raised shadow. Retired: define-time layer shape validation; api.md and the skinning guide do not promise it. Source: `weakC/themepkg.result.json`.
- `themes-P4-20` (Per-layer output carries the package's values and suppresses the native plate). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Every layer carries its own asset, scale type, slice centre, slice scale and tile size in array order; per-state maps swap only their layers; facet-skinned suppresses the plate and outranks every fill rule; the tag goes with the art. Retired: corner and outline suppression and per-layer tint rules of the deleted sheet model. Source: `weakC/themepkg.result.json`.
- `themes-P4-24` (v2 value-display and toggle slots are consumed (bar track/fill/cap/center, stepper plate, toggle track/knob)). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Stretchers (barTrack, barFill, toggleTrack, toggleKnob, stepperPlate) fill their part and follow the asset slice; barCap and barCenter keep their declared fixed box when the value changes. A per-slot whole-image classification is retired: docs/guide/10-rich-skinning.md makes slicing follow the asset slice rectangle. Source: `weakB/result-controls-values.json`.
- `themes-P4-48` (Per-state map compiles and a layer takes the same grammar). Status after strengthening: full. Note: A full and a partial state map give the same art on a recipe and on a layer for all five states (unstated states use default); a Button drives selected, hover, pressed and disabled, and disabled beats pressed. Retired: the canonical normalized map and the error-state tag. Source: `weakC/themepkg.result.json`.
- `themes-P5-02` (Reference packages compile to a complete native rule set). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Two compiles of each reference package give identical selector, priority and property lists (over 30 rules); the unnamed sheet paints the default palette; each palette paints distinct colors with the same rule count. Retired: the sheet_model stamp and schema. Source: `weakC/themepkg.result.json`.
- `themes-P5-11` (checkCoverage: covering/uncovering packages, silent compile of namespaced roles). Status after strengthening: full. Note: custom_control and ornate_gauge needs against glossy_mobile, classic_desktop, fantasy_ornate, pixel_quest and glossy_touch, documented color and fields needs on all packages, custom_control.check messages name the package id and role, silent define of namespaced roles. Retired: framework checkCoverage messages naming the package id; api.md does not promise it. Source: `weakC/themepkg.result.json`.
- `themes-P5-14` (Success and warning pairs clear 4.5:1 in every shipped variant). Status after strengthening: full. Note: All 13 shipped variants declare their own success/warning pairs, clear 4.5:1 with the worst pinned (fantasy_parchment/Daylight warning 5.27), are not the accent/content/each other, report entries are ok, and status rules paint them. Retired: the Facet Neutral Light variant, which is no longer shipped. Source: `weakC/themepkg.result.json`.
- `themes-P5-18` (Package icons resolve to art; unmapped names fall to framework art). Status after strengthening: full. Note: Ornate: 6 mapped + 1 namespaced resolve to its own asset content, unmapped menu and more to exact framework art; glossy_touch and compact_pointer declare only facet:search and take exact framework art for increment, decrement and chevron.trailing; retired: ASCII glyph floor. Source: `weakC/icons.result.json`.

## Retirement reasons in the coverage ledgers

The `tools/lune/coverage_*.json` files are the older, spec-level ledger. They give one reason for each retired spec or group of specs.

- `coverage_architecture.json`: 34 retired assertions inside replacement entries (`replacements.*.retiredAssertions`).
- `coverage_collections.json` spec `row_actions_dump_seam`: Dump/dependency-schema source scans described the deleted imperative RowActions engine and its diagnostic facade.
- `coverage_collections.json` spec `row_actions_state`: The separate pointer-state machine, probe radius transport and Facet capture arbitration are replaced with engine UIDragDetector plus local control policy.
- `coverage_collections.json` spec `table_rows_seam`: Source-level dependency surface checks described the deleted table_rows blueprint builder.
- `coverage_collections.json` spec `virtual_extents`: Facet prefix sums, extent indexes and window arithmetic are deleted; Compose.OrderedCollection owns these algorithms.
- `coverage_collections.json` spec `virtual_list_hosted_seam`: Removed controller/contribution attachment plumbing; native controls mount directly into the Compose Roblox runtime.
- `coverage_collections.json` spec `virtual_list_slot_guard`: The Facet blueprint/solver fill-slot refusal is removed. Native instances own sizing; measured content is observed through AbsoluteSize.
- `coverage_collections.json`: 75 retired assertions inside replacement entries (`replacements.*.retiredAssertions`).
- `coverage_gallery.json`: 26 retired assertions inside replacement entries (`replacements.*.retiredAssertions`).
- `coverage_hud.json`: 20 retired assertions inside replacement entries (`replacements.*.retiredAssertions`).
- `coverage_inputs.json` spec `activation_gate`: Generic activation-interception metadata and nested gate traversal were removed with scene composition. Applications express wake/unlock state using ordinary native button callbacks and Compose-controlled presentation.
- `coverage_inputs.json` spec `interaction_tokens`: Deleted shared gesture-classifier/token/velocity facade. Native engine owns gesture transport; the few retained control-specific thresholds and velocity decisions are private policies exercised by native collections tests.
- `coverage_inputs.json` spec `platform_env_binding`: Deleted synthetic environment binding, raw-input primacy overrides, camera/inset coherence belt and platform probe subscriptions. Consumers bind native services and properties directly through Compose.
- `coverage_inputs.json` spec `tap_arm_seam`: All assertions inspect deleted renderer tap-arm dependency records, echo timestamps, metadata dispatch and private armed-session storage. Native controls do not retain that renderer seam.
- `coverage_inputs.json`: 61 retired assertions inside replacement entries (`replacements.*.retiredAssertions`).
- `coverage_mechanisms.json` group `third-reviewed-extraction-and-adapter-mechanisms` (11 specs): Root approved exact thirdReview case inventory; old renderer extraction seams, adapter/style switches, core harness and schema registration were deleted. Native registration/documentation consistency requires separate native contract checks.
- `coverage_mechanisms.json` group `removed-layout-solver-policy-and-diagnostic-contracts` (12 specs): Assertions invoke the deleted Facet solver/environment/blueprint tree, solved rect diagnostics, partial-arrangement counters, waiver sweeps, region negotiation and distance-scale policies. Native Roblox layout and Insets/TextSize replace these mechanisms; this retirement does not claim native pixel geometry equivalence.
- `coverage_mechanisms.json` group `removed-pure-focus-graph-algorithms` (2 specs): Assertions address the removed Facet FocusGraph string-path/group topology API and its pure traversal oracle. Native GuiService selection and Compose collection focus use actual instances and separate behavioral cases.
- `coverage_mechanisms.json` group `removed-old-maintainer-and-authoring-scanners` (2 specs): Deleted scanners require semantic blueprint sizing, token/layout catalogs, old source module inventories and their maintainer obligations. The new native registration/docs contract, import graph, package purity and example builds replace these structural checks.
- `coverage_mechanisms.json` group `removed-text-modifier-facades` (4 specs): Deleted generic UI.textReveal/UI.valueReveal blueprint modifiers, grapheme-state renderer annotation and delayed overflow disclosure manager. Native TextBox/TextLabel properties, caller Compose animation and control-specific full-value disclosure are the current surface; no compatibility facade or old timing manager remains.
- `coverage_mechanisms.json` group `removed-toast-composition-service` (4 specs): Deleted application/presenter toast scheduler, global layer seam and blueprint presentation options. Compose/Engine own presentation lifetimes; Facet no longer owns a global toast composition service.
- `coverage_mechanisms.json` group `removed-theme-inference-and-glyph-shims` (2 specs): Deleted icon ASCII namespace glyph derivation and custom asset decode/inference/failure-ledger mechanisms. Native ImageLabel content and explicit theme asset data replace them.
- `coverage_mechanisms.json` group `removed-primitive-and-host-policy-shims` (3 specs): Divider was a Facet primitive facade (native Frame/style now); matrix_rows selected old simulator presets and asserted deleted Facet environment distance/size classes; mouse_scroll implemented deleted custom scroller pointer/paging shim (native ScrollingFrame/UIPageLayout now). No old facade is preserved.
- `coverage_native_paint.json`: 22 retired assertions inside replacement entries (`replacements.*.retiredAssertions`).
- `coverage_navigation.json` spec `backdrop_fade`: Retires the deleted scene/presenter backdrop-opacity diagnostic; native Alert presence, modal scrim ownership and interrupted transitions are exercised by native_navigation source-transition and dismissal cases.
- `coverage_navigation.json` spec `nav_bar`: Retires the deleted NavigationBar scaffolding constructor and scene geometry assertions; native NavigationStack and TabView own their control chrome, with retained destinations, native Back routing and responsive accessory geometry covered by native_navigation.
- `coverage_navigation.json`: 98 retired assertions inside replacement entries (`replacements.*.retiredAssertions`).
- `coverage_performance.json`: 4 retired assertions inside replacement entries (`replacements.*.retiredAssertions`).
- `coverage_radial.json`: 8 retired assertions inside replacement entries (`replacements.*.retiredAssertions`).
- `coverage_reference_apps.json`: 27 retired assertions inside replacement entries (`replacements.*.retiredAssertions`).
- `coverage_surface.json`: 147 retired assertions inside replacement entries (`replacements.*.retiredAssertions`).
- `coverage_themes_media.json` spec `theme_controller`: Facet install/swap/uninstall controller and its adapter/core settlement lifecycle are deleted. Native StyleLink and reactive StyleSheet are caller-owned Compose nodes.
- `coverage_themes_media.json` spec `theme_drift`: Source-scanning the removed theme-controller and solver constants is obsolete; new architecture boundary checks inspect the native surface.
- `coverage_themes_media.json` spec `theme_paint_repaint`: Renderer dirty/paint operation ordering and adapter fallback repaint paths are deleted. Native StyleRule properties are now materialized directly.
- `coverage_themes_media.json` spec `theme_pixel_content`: Solver-wide snapping of application content and renderer decoration hints are removed. Native image resampling remains a control/theme concern.
- `coverage_themes_media.json` spec `theme_roles`: Custom solver/renderer role expansion, calibrated text-metrics keys and exact Facet geometry fixtures are removed. Native styles and engine text/layout are authoritative.
- `coverage_themes_media.json` spec `theme_selectby`: Imperative theme-controller selection arbitration is removed. Callers select package readables through Compose formulas.
- `coverage_themes_media.json` spec `theme_snapshot`: Frozen environment metric snapshots, solver scaling and metric path resolution façade are removed. Controls read package policy directly; engine computes geometry.
- `coverage_virtual_monitors.json`: 15 retired assertions inside replacement entries (`replacements.*.retiredAssertions`).

## Use this page

- To accept a retirement, remove the promise from `docs/reference/api.md` and the guides, and record it in `CHANGELOG.md`.
- To restore a behavior, add the candidate case id to the contract in `tools/lune/verification_parity.json` and change its class to `a`.
- See [Verification parity](20-verification-parity.md) for the method and the class meanings.
